########################################
# Unique Bookmark Intro Events
########################################

namespace = war_unique_intro_events
normal_or_historical_nations = yes

### Wissenland Intro Event
# Wissenland must turn provincial suspicion, Nuln's guns, and Imperial politics into a coherent defense.
country_event = {
	id = war_unique_intro_events.1
	title = war_unique_intro_events.1.t
	desc = war_unique_intro_events.1.d
	picture = EMPIRE_eventPicture

	fire_only_once = no
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = A02
		NOT = { has_country_flag = did_intro_event }
	}

	immediate = { set_country_flag = did_intro_event }

# Hold Wissenland together.
	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		name = war_unique_intro_events.1.a
		add_army_professionalism = 0.05
		add_mercantilism = 5
	}
}

	}
}

### Top Knotz Intro Event
# The Top Knotz see tomb roads, desert cities, and caravan paths as the start of a hotter WAAAGH.
country_event = {
	id = war_unique_intro_events.9
	title = war_unique_intro_events.9.t
	desc = war_unique_intro_events.9.d
	picture = GREENSKIN_eventPicture

	fire_only_once = no
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { is_year = 2503 }
		tag = G04
		NOT = { has_country_flag = did_intro_event }
	}

	immediate = { set_country_flag = did_intro_event }

# Find a fight.
	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		name = war_unique_intro_events.9.a

		add_yearly_manpower = 2.0
		add_army_tradition = 50

		if = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					has_government_mechanic = orc_fightiness_mechanic
				}
			}
			add_government_power = {
				mechanic_type = orc_fightiness_mechanic
				power_type = orc_fightiness_power
				value = -25
			}
		}
	}
}

### Northern Provinces Intro Event
# The Northern Provinces hold the Bastion while Cathayan protocol strains around Miao Ying's command.
country_event = {
	id = war_unique_intro_events.19
	title = war_unique_intro_events.19.t
	desc = war_unique_intro_events.19.d
	picture = GRAND_CATHAY_eventPicture

	fire_only_once = no
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = I21
		NOT = { has_country_flag = did_cathay_intro_event }
	}

	immediate = { set_country_flag = did_cathay_intro_event }

# Guard the Bastion.
	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		name = war_unique_intro_events.19.a

		custom_tooltip = claim_all_cathay_tt

		hidden_effect = {
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					is_wasteland = no
					superregion = cathay_superregion
				}
				add_permanent_claim = ROOT
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					tag = I25
					is_subject_of = I25
				}
			}

			change_estate_land_share = {
				estate = estate_nobles
				share = 10
			}
			change_estate_land_share = {
				estate = estate_church
				share = 10
			}
			change_estate_land_share = {
				estate = estate_burghers
				share = 10
			}
			change_estate_land_share = {
				estate = estate_eunuchs
				share = 10
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = war_unique_intro_events.216
	title = war_unique_intro_events.216.t
	desc = war_unique_intro_events.216.d
	picture = NURGLE_eventPicture

	fire_only_once = no
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = B87
		NOT = { has_country_flag = did_intro_event }
	}

	immediate = { set_country_flag = did_intro_event }

# Ride the blessed rot.
	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		name = war_unique_intro_events.216.a

		add_army_tradition = 10
		add_church_power = 100
	}
}

### Bloodgrin Tribe Intro Event
# The Bloodgrin Tribe must turn orcish appetite, laughter, and a need for constant fighting into a WAAAGH.
country_event = {
	id = war_unique_intro_events.227
	title = war_unique_intro_events.227.t
	desc = war_unique_intro_events.227.d
	picture = GREENSKIN_eventPicture

	fire_only_once = no
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { is_year = 2503 }
		tag = G01
		NOT = { has_country_flag = did_intro_event }
	}

	immediate = { set_country_flag = did_intro_event }

# Grin through the blood.
	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		name = war_unique_intro_events.227.a

		add_yearly_manpower = 2.0
		add_army_tradition = 50
		if = {
					limit = {
						owner = {
							has_government_mechanic = orc_fightiness_mechanic
						}
					}
					add_government_power = {
						mechanic_type = orc_fightiness_mechanic
						power_type = orc_fightiness_power
						value = -25
					}
				}
	}
}

### Western Provinces Intro Event
# The Western Provinces must harden frontier command, caravan defense, and court legitimacy into Cathayan authority.
country_event = {
	id = war_unique_intro_events.317
	title = war_unique_intro_events.317.t
	desc = war_unique_intro_events.317.d
	picture = GRAND_CATHAY_eventPicture

	fire_only_once = no
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = I22
		NOT = { has_country_flag = did_cathay_intro_event }
	}

	immediate = { set_country_flag = did_cathay_intro_event }

# Temper the west.
	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		name = war_unique_intro_events.317.a

		custom_tooltip = claim_all_cathay_tt

		hidden_effect = {
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					is_wasteland = no
					superregion = cathay_superregion
				}
				add_permanent_claim = ROOT
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					tag = I25
					is_subject_of = I25
				}
			}

			change_estate_land_share = {
				estate = estate_nobles
				share = 10
			}
			change_estate_land_share = {
				estate = estate_church
				share = 10
			}
			change_estate_land_share = {
				estate = estate_burghers
				share = 10
			}
			change_estate_land_share = {
				estate = estate_eunuchs
				share = 10
			}
		}
	}
}

# The Burning Wind Nomads must reconcile steppe mobility, Cathayan service, and frontier suspicion.

	id = war_unique_intro_events.320
	title = war_unique_intro_events.320.t
	desc = war_unique_intro_events.320.d
	picture = GRAND_CATHAY_eventPicture

	fire_only_once = no
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = I28
		NOT = { has_country_flag = did_cathay_intro_event }
	}

	immediate = { set_country_flag = did_cathay_intro_event }

# Ride the burning wind.
	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		name = war_unique_intro_events.320.a

		custom_tooltip = claim_all_cathay_tt

		hidden_effect = {
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					is_wasteland = no
					superregion = cathay_superregion
				}
				add_permanent_claim = ROOT
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					tag = I25
					is_subject_of = I25
				}
			}

			change_estate_land_share = {
				estate = estate_nobles
				share = 10
			}
			change_estate_land_share = {
				estate = estate_church
				share = 10
			}
			change_estate_land_share = {
				estate = estate_burghers
				share = 10
			}
			change_estate_land_share = {
				estate = estate_eunuchs
				share = 10
			}
		}
	}
}

### Grand Cathay Intro Event
# Grand Cathay must restore dragon authority, dominion discipline, and celestial order across a fractured empire.
country_event = {
	id = war_unique_intro_events.347
	title = war_unique_intro_events.347.t
	desc = war_unique_intro_events.347.d
	picture = GRAND_CATHAY_eventPicture

	fire_only_once = no
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = I99
		NOT = { has_country_flag = did_cathay_intro_event }
	}

	immediate = { set_country_flag = did_cathay_intro_event }

# Restore celestial order.
	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		name = war_unique_intro_events.347.a

		custom_tooltip = claim_all_cathay_tt

		hidden_effect = {
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					is_wasteland = no
					superregion = cathay_superregion
				}
				add_permanent_claim = ROOT
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					tag = I25
					is_subject_of = I25
				}
			}

			change_estate_land_share = {
				estate = estate_nobles
				share = 10
			}
			change_estate_land_share = {
				estate = estate_church
				share = 10
			}
			change_estate_land_share = {
				estate = estate_burghers
				share = 10
			}
			change_estate_land_share = {
				estate = estate_eunuchs
				share = 10
			}
		}
	}
}
